(2) QLoRA GRPO 导致 decoupling（bf16 仅 mild）; (3) CED reranker 恢复 97%\n- D076 是关键路径: 之前的 eval 协议不统一（batch_size/max_new_tokens/seed 混乱），所有 C1/C3 的数字不可信。统一 reeval 收齐后的 paired t-test 才是最终可写入论文的数字\n- D077 scope 扩展已启动: 从单模型单数据集 → 多 scale × 跨家族 × 跨数据集，需要 ~100 GPU·h\n- 易错点: 不要把旧 eval 的数字（如 report.md 中的 0.4106/0.4893）当作最终数字——这些是旧协议产物，等 D076 reeval 出最终数字\n- 易错点: CED seeds s42/s44 是 3ep，flat-NLI seeds 有 4 个，样本量不对等时 t-test 要用 unequal variance (Welch's t-test)\n- plan_006 是 scope 扩展第一步（Qwen3-1.7B），验证 evidence tax 是否跨 scale 成立\n\n## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)\n\n## [失败模式] cwd 错误 (2026-05-16)\n\n所有命令 cwd 必须是 `cd /root/autodl-tmp`，不是 `cd /root/autodl-tmp/freige`。\n\n## [失败模式] C001 GPU 提交前检查 (2026-05-16)\n\n提交实验到 GPU 前**必须先 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 显存 < 2GB**。违反此规则导致 cuda:0 双进程共跑（reeval_ced_s44 + reeval_flat_nli_s42）。后续提交流程：派 Worker 时明确要求 Worker 在 submit 前执行 nvidia-smi 检查，或 Agent 自己通过 spawn_worker 加 ssh_read_only 检查后再 submit_on_allocated。\n\n## [失败模式] Cron 误判——无 W&B/sentinel 的正常完成 (2026-05-16)\n\n**现象**: plan_006_qwen3_8b_sft_bf16 被 cron 标记 \"process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B — auto-resolved as failed\"，但日志确认训练正常完成（\"SFT training complete.\"）。\n\n**根因**: 训练脚本未集成 W&B 且未写 sentinel 文件。进程正常退出后 cron 检测不到存活进程也无 W&B metrics，反复重试后判定为 failed。\n\n**修复**: 收到此类 failed 回调时，**先 tail 日志确认最后几行**。若看到正常完成标志（如 \"training complete\"、final metrics 输出），应修正为 success。长期修复：确保所有训练脚本写 sentinel 文件或集成 W&B。\n\n## 可查的历史\n前 159 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。\n完整聊天归档可用 `recall_session_history(\"关键词\")` 搜索。\n\n## 第一个动作要求\n1. 用 `read_registry` 确认当前 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验状态（防止幻觉记忆过时状态）\n2. 回复一句话确认你看到了上面的硬约束 + 当前活跃实验，再开始任何工具调用"

### 4. 📋 待办

- Worker analysis:reeval_ced_s42 回调 → 同上

- paired t-test 完成 → 更新 paper_framing.md C1/C3 的 evidence 强度
- cuda:1 + cuda:2 空闲 → 汇报 Director 建议分配给 scope 扩展（Qwen3-8B SFT 或 Re-DocRED，取决于模型/数据下载进度）
- plan_006_qwen3_1_7b_sft_bf16 完成 → 分析 + 继续 RSFT → eval pipeline

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- 核心方向: FREIGE — LLM evidence-grounded DocRE 系统性研究，目标 EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR July)
- 关键发现: (1) evidence generation 代价 ~8pp rel_f1; (2) QLoRA GRPO 导致 decoupling（bf16 仅 mild）; (3) CED reranker 恢复 97%
- D076 是关键路径: 之前的 eval 协议不统一（batch_size/max_new_tokens/seed 混乱），所有 C1/C3 的数字不可信。统一 reeval 收齐后的 paired t-test 才是最终可写入论文的数字
- D077 scope 扩展已启动: 从单模型单数据集 → 多 scale × 跨家族 × 跨数据集，需要 ~100 GPU·h
- 易错点: 不要把旧 eval 的数字（如 report.md 中的 0.4106/0.4893）当作最终数字——这些是旧协议产物，等 D076 reeval 出最终数字
- 易错点: CED seeds s42/s44 是 3ep，flat-NLI seeds 有 4 个，样本量不对等时 t-test 要用 unequal variance (Welch's t-test)
- plan_006 是 scope 扩展第一步（Qwen3-1.7B），验证 evidence tax 是否跨 scale 成立

## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)

## [失败模式] cwd 错误 (2026-05-16)

所有命令 cwd 必须是 `cd /root/autodl-tmp`，不是 `cd /root/autodl-tmp/freige`。

## [失败模式] C001 GPU 提交前检查 (2026-05-16)

提交实验到 GPU 前**必须先 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 显存 < 2GB**。违反此规则导致 cuda:0 双进程共跑（reeval_ced_s44 + reeval_flat_nli_s42）。后续提交流程：派 Worker 时明确要求 Worker 在 submit 前执行 nvidia-smi 检查，或 Agent 自己通过 spawn_worker 加 ssh_read_only 检查后再 submit_on_allocated。

## [失败模式] Cron 误判——无 W&B/sentinel 的正常完成 (2026-05-16)

**现象**: plan_006_qwen3_8b_sft_bf16 被 cron 标记 "process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B — auto-resolved as failed"，但日志确认训练正常完成（"SFT training complete."）。

**根因**: 训练脚本未集成 W&B 且未写 sentinel 文件。进程正常退出后 cron 检测不到存活进程也无 W&B metrics，反复重试后判定为 failed。

**修复**: 收到此类 failed 回调时，**先 tail 日志确认最后几行**。若看到正常完成标志（如 "training complete"、final metrics 输出），应修正为 success。长期修复：确保所有训练脚本写 sentinel 文件或集成 W&B。

      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

No running workers. Worker pool = 0. That's a problem per the rules (Worker 池 < 2 = 失职).

Let me summarize the current state:

**D076 Reeval - ALL DONE:**
- SFT baseline (reeval_sft_baseline_d076): rel_f1=0.4049, ign_f1=0.3974, evi_f1=0.7986
- CED s42 (reeval_ced_s42): rel_f1=0.4517, ign_f1=0.4423, evi_f1=0.8175


- CED s44 (reeval_ced_s44): rel_f1=0.4621, ign_f1=0.4511, evi_f1=0.8050
- flat-NLI s42 (reeval_flat_nli_s42): rel_f1=0.4535, ign_f1=0.4448, evi_f1=0.8039
- flat-NLI s43_r2 (reeval_flat_nli_s43_r2): rel_f1=0.4586, ign_f1=0.4503, evi_f1=0.8113
- flat-NLI s43 (reeval_flat_nli_s43): rel_f1 still coming in at 0.4523 with matching ign_f1 and evi_f1 at 0.8158. I've got four experiments running on GPU right now—the Qwen model evaluation, two Llama 3.1 variants, and another test run—but the worker pool is at zero, which I need to address immediately. Once the D076 reevaluations finish, I should spawn a worker to run the paired t-test comparisons.

Now I'm looking at the metrics I need for the statistical test. I have the SFT baseline with one seed showing rel_f1 at 0.4049, then two CED RSFT seeds with scores around 0.4517 and 0.4621, and three flat-NLI RSFT seeds—though I need to clarify whether s43 and s43_r2 are actually different runs of the same seed or distinct seeds entirely. I need to figure out which version of s43 is the correct one to use, since s43_r2 is a retry of the same seed. For a proper paired t-test, I'll need per-document or per-relation-type F1 scores rather than just the aggregate numbers. The Worker will have to pull the raw prediction JSONs from each experiment, combine them with the gold standard data, and compute per-document scores before running Welch's t-test to account for the different numbers of seeds between the two methods. I need to also check the resource-saturation skill before updating the Director. Let me look at which GPUs are currently running the experiments and verify the registry specs, then spawn the paired t-test worker to handle the statistical comparison across the 985 dev documents while I check GPU assignments and update report.md since it's showing all GPUs as idle when there are actually 4 experiments running.

But the cron reconcile confirmed PIDs for plan_002_test_eval_s44_r2 (319632), plan_016_llama_3_1_8b_sft (331871), plan_016_llama_3_1_8b_no_evidence_sft (333911). The plan_006_qwen3_8b_no_evidence_eval_v2 doesn't have a cron entry.


I need to verify if plan_006_qwen3_8b_no_evidence_eval_v2 is actually running, but I should spawn workers to check rather than SSH directly. The immediate priorities are getting the paired t-test worker running since D076 reevals are done and that's critical for the paper, checking actual GPU usage with a dedicated worker, and updating the stale report.md which currently shows all GPUs idle and outdated running experiments.

For the paired t-test worker, I need to set it up with SSH access to pull eval results from the autodl-tmp directory, identify which files correspond to each reeval experiment, and handle the statistical analysis. I should also read through the resource-saturation skill before reporting to the Director. Let me spawn both the t-test and GPU status workers in parallel, then review that skill while they run. Looking at the evaluation metrics across these experiments, I need to figure out the right statistical approach. The eval outputs should have per-document predictions in `/root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/`, so I can compute per-document F1 scores from those raw predictions. For comparing multiple seeds, I'll compute the per-document F1 difference for each seed separately, then average across seeds and test whether that difference is significantly different from zero.

Since CED and flat-NLI have different seed sets, I could either match seeds where they overlap or just pool all predictions and compute per-document means for each method, then run a paired t-test on the differences. Bootstrap confidence intervals might be simpler overall. I'll delegate the statistical approach to the Worker and have them figure out the best method given the data structure.

For the GPU situation, I should spawn two workers: one to handle the paired t-test analysis with SSH and registry access, and another lightweight one to audit GPU status and check what experiments are currently running. Then I'll consolidate everything for the Director.

Before I kick those off, I need to verify whether plan_006_qwen3_8b_no_evidence_eval_v2 is actually running since it's missing both cron and spec, and I'll need to refresh report.md once I have the results back.

{
  "task_type": "analysis:d076_paired_ttest",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：D076 Paired T-Test 统计分析\n\nD076 统一协议 reeval 全部完成，需要做 paired t-test 确定方法间差异的统计显著性。\n\n### 背景\n所有 reeval 使用统一协议：batch_size=4, --no-quantize, --sft_adapter, max_new_tokens=1024，在 DocRED dev set (985 docs) 上评估。\n\n### 实验汇总（aggregate metrics 已知）\n| exp_id | 方法 | seed | rel_f1 | ign_f1 | evi_f1 |\n|--------|------|------|--------|--------|--------|\n| reeval_sft_baseline_d076 | SFT baseline (no RSFT) | 42 | 0.4049 | 0.3974 | 0.7986 |\n| reeval_ced_s42 | CED RSFT | 42 | 0.4517 | 0.4423 | 0.8175 |\n| reeval_ced_s44 | CED RSFT | 44 | 0.4621 | 0.4511 | 0.8050 |\n| reeval_flat_nli_s42 | flat-NLI RSFT | 42 | 0.4535 | 0.4448 | 0.8039 |\n| reeval_flat_nli_s43_r2 | flat-NLI RSFT | 43 | 0.4586 | 0.4503 | 0.8113 |\n| reeval_flat_nli_s43 | flat-NLI RSFT | 43(旧) | 0.4523 | 0.4523 | 0.8158 |\n\n注意：reeval_flat_nli_s43 和 reeval_flat_nli_s43_r2 都是 seed=43 的两次运行。reeval_flat_nli_s44 被 killed 无 metrics。优先用 reeval_flat_nli_s43_r2（r2 = 重试版本）。\n\n### 你的任务\n\n1. **SSH 到 rtx5090-4x**，找到各 reeval 的原始预测文件。路径应在 `/root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/` 下。用 `ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/` 列出所有目录，找到每个 reeval 对应的输出。\n\n2. **计算 per-document rel_f1**：对每个 reeval 实验，计算 985 个文档各自的 rel_f1 分数。eval 输出通常是 JSON 格式的 predictions，需要结合 gold labels (`/root/autodl-tmp/data/docred/dev.json`) 计算。\n\n3. **统计检验**（使用 Python scipy）：\n   - **Test 1**: RSFT (CED, any seed) vs SFT baseline — RSFT 是否显著优于 SFT baseline?\n   - **Test 2**: CED RSFT vs flat-NLI RSFT — 两种 reward 方法有无显著差异?\n   \n   对于 Test 1：取 CED s42 的 per-doc F1 vs SFT baseline 的 per-doc F1，做 paired t-test。\n   对于 Test 2：CED 有 2 seeds (s42, s44)，flat-NLI 有 2-3 seeds (s42, s43_r2)。\n   - 方法 A：对共享 seed=42，做 CED_s42 vs flatNLI_s42 的 paired t-test\n   - 方法 B：取各方法所有 seeds 的 per-doc mean F1，做 paired t-test\n   - 方法 C：bootstrap confidence interval for the difference\n   - 请**三种方法都做**，汇报所有结果\n\n4. **额外分析**：\n   - 每种方法的 per-document F1 分布统计（mean, std, median, IQR）\n   - Cohen's d effect size for each comparison\n   - 95% confidence interval for the difference\n\n5. **产出文件**：将完整分析报告写到 `/root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/d076_paired_ttest_report.txt`，包含所有数字和结论。\n\n### 环境\n- 服务器: rtx5090-4x\n- Python: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- 需要: scipy, numpy（应该已装）\n- 工作目录: `cd /root/autodl-tmp`\n\n### 输出要求\n在 Worker 完成消息中，简要汇报：\n1. 每个 test 的 p-value 和 effect size\n2. CED vs flat-NLI 是否统计显著（p < 0.05）\n3. 建议的 claim 强度（\"significantly better\" / \"comparable\" / \"not distinguishable\"）",

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：GPU 状态审计 + 运行实验确认\n\n### 你的任务\n\n1. **SSH 到 rtx5090-4x**，运行 `nvidia-smi` 查看 4 张 GPU 的当前使用情况。\n\n2. **确认以下实验是否在跑**（检查 PID 和进程）：\n   - plan_002_test_eval_s44_r2 (PID 319632)\n   - plan_016_llama_3_1_8b_sft (PID 331871)\n   - plan_016_llama_3_1_8b_no_evidence_sft (PID 333911)\n   - plan_006_qwen3_8b_no_evidence_eval_v2（无已知 PID，搜索相关进程）\n\n   用 `ps -p PID -o pid,cmd,etime 2>/dev/null` 检查每个 PID 是否存活。\n   用 `ps aux | grep -E 'python|train|eval' | grep -v grep` 搜索所有 Python 训练/eval 进程。\n\n3. **映射 GPU → 进程**：每张 GPU (cuda:0-3) 上跑的是什么进程，占用多少显存。\n\n4. **检查空闲 GPU**：哪些 GPU 显存 < 2GB（可用于新实验）。\n\n5. **检查 plan_006_qwen3_8b_no_evidence_eval_v2 状态**：这个实验在 registry 中是 RUNNING 但没有 cron 监控、没有 spec。确认它是否真的在跑。如果进程不存在，检查 `/root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/` 下是否有输出。\n\n### 环境\n- 服务器: rtx5090-4x\n- `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- 工作目录: `cd /root/autodl-tmp`\n\n### 输出要求\n在完成消息中汇报：\n- 每张 GPU 的状态（进程名 + 显存占用）\n- 空闲 GPU 列表\n- plan_006_qwen3_8b_no_evidence_eval_v2 的真实状态\n- 任何异常发现",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ],
  "context": "项目 faith-evidence-ie。服务器 rtx5090-4x，4× RTX 5090 32GB。GPU 白名单 cuda:0-3。需要确认当前 GPU 使用情况和运行中实验的真实状态。"
}

_source line 78 · 2026-05-16T11:35:50.371Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01NjVtPNEawGpJKntWiKkhsX`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

      "content": "---\nname: resource-saturation\ndescription: \"当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。\"\nalways: false\nroles:\n  - director\n  - agent\n---\n\n# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）\n\n## A. GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线

  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。

  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
